# The Beasts of Daniel & Revelation

**[00:00:00]** The books of Daniel and Revelation are famous for visions of terrible beasts that are persecuting God's people all the way through the return of Christ.

**[00:00:07]** In this episode, we're going to look at who these beasts are and what these visions have to say about our understanding of what's soon to come.

**[00:00:23]** Hey, what's up everybody? Welcome back to the show. Thanks so much for being with me today.

**[00:00:26]** If you haven't yet subscribed, make sure you do so and do it on my website, danceoflife.com.

**[00:00:31]** Just in case something happens with these platforms, you just never know. That's the best way to stay in touch.

**[00:00:36]** But today we're jumping into both the books of Daniel and Revelation.

**[00:00:40]** If you've been with me so far, we have been ping-ponging between these two books because they really are teaming up together to give us a whole picture of the end times.

**[00:00:50]** If you recall, there's so many similarities between the books of Daniel and Revelation.

**[00:00:55]** Truly, Revelation...

**[00:00:56]** can't be understood without understanding Daniel.

**[00:01:00]** And so we have to look at both and we have to understand both in context of one another.

**[00:01:04]** Now, of course, Revelation was written after the book of Daniel,

**[00:01:08]** but it builds off of a lot of things in Daniel and many things that Daniel saw in his visions, even though he didn't really fully understand them.

**[00:01:16]** They relate to things that John saw later because ultimately they're painting a picture of the same timeline of events,
